| ENG-288 | Atomic replay transition + mid-retry guard (fourth-wave Q-02) | Shipped | P1 | — | Promotes fourth-wave-audit Q-02 + red-team corrections C-2/C-9. **Problem:** `replayDeadLetter` ran `resetRunForReplay` → `markNodeQueued` → `enqueueNode` as three separate awaits; a cancellation landing between the reset and the queue left a `queued` node on a `cancelled` run that the runtime guard skips forever — silent false recovery. Separately, `/dlq/replay` accepted a node currently `queued` mid-engine-retry (double side-effect risk). **Shipped:** new `claimReplayTransition(runId, nodeId)` in `persistence.ts` does the un-terminate + re-queue in ONE `db.transaction` — flips `failed→running` (idempotent no-op when already `running`: the multi-dead-letter-same-run case), reads the run status back inside the tx and throws `ReplayNotClaimableError('run_not_replayable')` for anything else (cancelled/succeeded — a deliberate behaviour change from the pre-Q-02 silent queue-on-terminal-run, per red-team #14), and throws `ReplayNotClaimableError('node_mid_retry')` when the node is already `queued`; both throws roll the tx back so no partial state survives. `replayDeadLetter` calls it **claim-first** (before the snapshot write) so a rejected claim mutates nothing — no snapshot under an in-flight retry, no duplicate job. The two single `/dlq/replay` paths map the error to 409 (`dlq_replay_conflict` / `dlq_node_mid_retry`, EN/ES); bulk + auto-healing paths already catch generically. Dead `resetRunForReplay` (now zero callers) removed. **New engine integration lane** (mirrors data/api: `vite.integration.config.ts` + `test:integration` script, excluded from the unit suite, run after the api lane in both `run-integration.mjs` + `run-e2e.mjs`) — the atomic CAS + rollback-on-throw can't be proven on a mocked DB. **Verified:** engine 76 files/971 unit (dlq-replay adapter test rewritten: claims-before-snapshot + rejected-claim-mutates-nothing) + **engine 4/4 integration against real Postgres** (happy path; cancelled→`run_not_replayable` rollback; **`node_mid_retry`→run stays `failed`, the flip rolled back** — the critical transaction proof; multi-DLQ already-running run claims its node); api 83/1097 (+2 route tests mapping each rejection to its 409 code); web 84/732 (i18n parity); `tsc --noEmit` clean. **Unblocks:** M-01 (redrive) per C-9. |
| ENG-289 | Enforce config.timeoutMs at the executor chokepoint (fourth-wave Q-01, re-scoped per red-team C-2) | Shipped | P1 | — | Promotes fourth-wave-audit Q-01 with the red-team C-2 correction. **Problem:** `core/timeout.ts` exports `withTimeout`/`getNodeTimeoutMs` and its docstring claimed `runtime.ts` used them — false; the only consumer was a PRIVATE duplicate in `node-registry.ts` (agent tool-loop). `execute-node.ts` awaited the executor unwrapped, so a hung `tool`/`subworkflow`/`ai`/`transform` node blocked the worker until the 5-minute stalled-node reaper. **C-2 correction:** the original spec (`withTimeout` = `Promise.race`) *abandons*, not cancels — a write-side node (email/POST/db.write) that times out keeps running and may complete its effect AFTER the node is marked failed → blind replay = double effect. **Shipped:** `withTimeout` now (a) swallows the abandoned promise's late rejection (no `unhandledRejection` after the race), (b) takes an `{ label }` opt folded into `NodeTimeoutError`'s message. `execute-node.ts` wraps the single executor call with `withTimeout(executor(...), getNodeTimeoutMs(node), { label: node.type })` — frees the worker at the deadline (throws `NodeTimeoutError` code `NODE_TIMEOUT` → normal failure → retry/DLQ), byte-for-byte pass-through when no timeout declared. **Write-side safety (C-2):** `NodeTimeoutError.writeSide` is set via new `isWriteSideNode(node)` (non-safe HTTP method / `writeSide` tool — same taxonomy the sandbox dry-run-skips) and rides through `redactError` → `SerializedError.writeSide` → `error_json`/DLQ so a possibly-committed effect isn't replayed blindly; http itself already self-cancels via `fetchHttpTarget`'s own `config.timeoutMs` abort. The private `withTimeout` duplicate removed (agent loop now uses the shared core impl); stale docstring fixed. **Follow-up noted:** gating the DLQ/auto-healing replay ELIGIBILITY on the `writeSide` flag (vs just surfacing it) composes with M-01 redrive — deferred. **Verified:** engine 76 files/976 (5 new execute-node tests: enforcement/NODE_TIMEOUT, writeSide true/false, no-timeout pass-through, and the late-rejection-swallow with a real `unhandledRejection` listener); `tsc --noEmit` clean on engine + api. |

Everything else that violated is **warn** (55 warnings = visible ratchet backlog: noUnsafeOptionalChaining 16, noUnusedVariables 12, noThenProperty 10, useIterableCallbackReturn 5, noUnusedFunctionParameters 4, noConfusingVoidType 3, noShadowRestrictedNames 2, noDoubleEquals 2, noImplicitAnyLet 1) — a follow-up promotes them to error as fixed. **Deferred by design:** the whole `a11y` domain is `preset: none` (123 violations) — it belongs to the dedicated W-02 (ModalShell focus trap) + WCAG gate ticket, which re-enables it; noNonNullAssertion/noExplicitAny/useExhaustiveDependencies/useImportType/useTemplate off (deliberate `!` idiom / the P-32 ratchet already governs `as any` / cosmetic mass-touch rules). **Verified:** `pnpm lint` exit 0; the 55-file mechanical cleanup is behavior-preserving — `pnpm build` green + `pnpm test` green byte-for-byte (engine 976, api 1097, web 732, data 364, shared 363, ai 152, mcp-server 66, sdk-node 62, solution-packs 8, db 4); `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` (CI's exact command) passes on the pinned pnpm 10.23.0. |

Callers that pinned `'runs'` pass it explicitly; the redundant `onOpenTab('runs')` after `onOpenRun` in RecoveryCenterTiles (queue-row + approvals-hold) is dropped (openRun sets it synchronously). (3) **i18n event catalog** — 9 lifecycle types the runtime emits (`node.running`/`node.retry`/`node.resumed`/`node.succeeded`/`decision.made`/`improvement.evaluated`/`rollback.triggered`/`rollback.completed`/`run.status_checked`) had NO `runEvents.*` label and rendered as raw strings on the timeline; added EN+ES, and moved the closed `WORKFLOW_EVENT_TYPES` catalogue to `@janusly/shared/src/run-events` (re-exported by `core/events.ts`) so a contract test in `server-events.test.ts` asserts every member is localised in both locales — drift now fails CI. (4) **Persisted terminal events** — `run.succeeded`/`run.failed` were dead catalog keys (terminal status was only an ephemeral SSE frame); `updateRunStatusFromNodes` now appends a persisted `run_events` row next to the status flip, via a conditional `ne(status)` CAS + `.returning()` so the append fires EXACTLY once even under concurrent node-completions. **Verified:** web 85 files/741 (new RunsPanel card test + Inspector failure-header test + i18n coverage contract); engine 976 unit + **new real-Postgres integration test** (`update-run-status.integration.test.ts`: succeeded→1 `run.succeeded`, failed→1 `run.failed`, second rollup→no duplicate); shared 363; api 1097; `tsc --noEmit` clean across shared/engine/web; `pnpm lint` exit 0. **Live smoke (dev stack + seeded failed run):** the failed-node card renders `edit_notion_page · attempt 3 · 42s` + the error message on the Runs tab (EN + ES `intento 3 · 42s`), 0 console errors; **live psql**: a fresh engine run persists `run.started→node.*→run.succeeded→run.status_checked` (status `succeeded`) and a failing run persists exactly one `run.failed`. |
| ENG-292 | Draft-safe authoring — dirty flag + confirm guards + local drafts + Cmd+S (fourth-wave S-01) | Shipped | P1 | — | Promotes fourth-wave-audit S-01, with one deliberate re-scope. **Problem:** an invalid draft was unsaveable (client gate at `saveWorkflow` + server 400 on `/workflows/save`), no `beforeunload` existed, and 6 paths replaced the canvas without confirm — and the audit's "`currentWorkflowSaved` already tracks the signal" claim was WRONG (that flag means "exists server-side" and never flips on edits), so the ticket first needed a real dirty flag. **Shipped:** (1) store `workflowDirty` — true on semantic mutations (addNode/name/config/type/edge-condition/connect/snippet-insert setNodes/setEdges + node/edge REMOVALS via the change reducers; position drags deliberately excluded — layout is never serialized), false on hydrate/new/markWorkflowSaved; `markWorkflowDirty()` for the restore path. (2) **Unsaved-work confirm guard** (shared `ConfirmDialog`, tone danger) on all 6 canvas-replacing paths: `openWorkflow`, AI-generate (guards BEFORE the LLM call — a declined confirm burns no tokens; contract now `Promise<…|null>`, copilot handles null by keeping the prompt), use-template, open-recipe, New ×2, and VersionHistoryPanel version-load. (3) **`beforeunload`** warns while dirty. (4) **Cmd/Ctrl+S** in `useKeyboardShortcuts` — fires even while typing in a field, always suppresses the browser save-page dialog; ⌘S row added to ShortcutsModal. (5) **Local draft substrate** (`useDraftPersistence.ts`): debounced (1s) localStorage autosave keyed `janusly:draft:<org>:<workflowId>` + a `latest` pointer; cleared ONLY on the dirty→clean transition of the SAME workflow id (a hydrate to another workflow keeps the outgoing draft as the crash-recovery copy; a replace mid-debounce cancels the pending write so old content never lands under the new id); restore prompts on `openWorkflow` (per-id) and once per app load (latest pointer — covers never-saved workflows whose random id isn't reachable from Flows); declining deletes the draft; restoring re-marks dirty. **Deliberate re-scope:** the audit sketch said server-side `status:'draft'` versions bypassing the validity gate — rejected because `workflow_versions` has no draft vocabulary and a semantically-invalid "latest" would poison rollback counting, schedule sync (`syncWorkflowSchedules` reads latest), and readiness; the localStorage substrate delivers the "never lose work" goal without touching the version table. A server draft lane remains available as its own ticket if multi-device drafts are ever needed. **Verified:** web 87 files/755 (14 new: 5 store dirty-transitions incl. position-vs-removal, 4 Cmd+S hook, 5 draft autosave/clear/crash-copy/mid-debounce/pointer); `pnpm lint` exit 0; vite build green. **Live smoke (real Chromium, EN+ES, 0 console errors):** palette-add → draft lands in localStorage (probe: 3 nodes + savedAt); Open flow while dirty → "Unsaved changes / Cambios sin guardar" dialog (screenshots both locales); Cancel keeps canvas + draft; Discard opens the target AND keeps the outgoing draft; reload → "Restore local draft? / ¿Restaurar borrador local?" mount prompt (screenshot) → Restore → canvas back to 3 nodes + `beforeunload` re-armed (probe `defaultPrevented=true`); ⌘S → browser dialog suppressed + **psql**: `workflow_versions` v1 of `ui-test` with exactly 3 nodes + draft cleared + beforeunload disarmed; ⌘S row visible in the shortcuts modal ("⌘S Guardar el workflow actual"). |
| ENG-293 | Suspect-version change correlation — "what changed?" on the DLQ detail (second-wave M-08) | Shipped | P1 | — | Promotes second-wave-audit M-08 ("what changed?" is the first RCA question in every incident tool; Janusly had version history + `computeWorkflowDiff` and never volunteered the answer). **Shipped:** read-time correlation, deliberately NOT a write-path tag (no migration, no new column, self-corrects as rows change): new `apps/api/src/suspect-version.ts` — `resolveSuspectVersion(orgId, runId, failureAt)` resolves the failing run's own `workflowVersionId`; when that version is v2+ (a predecessor exists to diff against) AND was saved within `SUSPECT_VERSION_WINDOW_MS` (24h) before the failure, returns `{workflowId, version, versionId, savedAt, previousVersion, previousVersionId, dagJson, previousDagJson}` — both DAG snapshots ride along so the web renders the diff with zero extra fetches. ### 3d. Open decisions blocking tickets

The following tickets are not in §3b because they wait on a strategic decision (see `docs/PLAN.md` §13). Move them into §3b once the decision is made.

- **Pricing model decision.** Affects `usage_events` shape (ENG-012). If we pick per-token, the row writes one shape; per-run, another. Currently writing both is fine — unblock when pricing is chosen.
- **License choice.** Affects MCP server bundling (ENG-013) and contributor pipeline (ENG-026 sub-item). Stays as a footnote on those tickets.
- **Self-host vs cloud canonical product.** Affects retention defaults (ENG-026) and SaaS-only flags. Not blocking ENG-008..023.
- **AI training data / memory rollout policy.** Affects future memory/RL tickets (ENG-025) and is tracked explicitly by ENG-114. The canonical policy lives in [`docs/memory-policy.md`](memory-policy.md); it covers eligibility, two-flag opt-in consent, per-kind retention, shipped purge paths, future delete/export admin surfaces, embedding provider posture, prompt-injection framing, the `org_configs.memory.*` catalog, audit actions, DPA language, and incident response. ENG-115/116 shipped persistent memory and recovery recall behind `JANUSLY_MEMORY_ENABLED` + tenant consent; broad customer rollout remains gated by the policy's Product / Legal / Engineering sign-off boxes. OpenAI-specific schema assumptions remain rejected.

---

## 4. How to add a ticket

1. Pick the next `ENG-NNN` (highest existing + 1).
2. Add the row to §3b with `Status: Pending` and a Scope cell that contains acceptance criteria.
3. If the work is part of a Phase from `docs/PLAN.md` §11, set `Phase` accordingly. If it's a standalone quick-win or fix, leave Phase as `—`.
4. If the ticket depends on another, mention the blocker in the Scope cell ("Depends on ENG-NNN").
5. If the ticket is blocked by an open question (`docs/PLAN.md` §13), set `Status: Gated` and reference the question in §3d.

When the implementer skill (`janus-ship`) closes a ticket, it appends a concise `**Summary:**`, sets Status to `Shipped`, and moves the row from §3b to §3c ARCHIVED. If work remains, keep it in §3b as `Partial` with an updated `**Remaining:**` line. The reviewer skill (`janus-review`) verifies the status and archive move are coherent with the staged diff.
